*    Well Rounded: This rotating stud extractor ensures a picture perfect fit around the damaged stud; This stud remover tool can handle studs 1/4"—1/2” (6MM—12MM) in size 
*    Fits Like a Glove: Turn to your stud remover when grappling with rusted bolts, stripped hardware, and chipped or broken nuts, BEST RESULTS WITH PENETRATING OIL OR HEAT 
*    Any Which Way: The square drive of the stud removal tool fits manual wrenches or ratchets; If you prefer flat or adjustable sockets, pliers, or wrenches, the hex end is for you 
*    Get a Grip: You won’t have to chase after runaway stud removers again; Knurling engraved on this bolt extractor will prevent your tools from slipping through your fingers 
*    Product Details: Universal stud extractor fits tools with 1/4"—1/2” (6—12MM) diameters; 3/8” hex end drive for universal wrench or ratchet attachment

## Product Description

The OEMTOOLS 25438 Universal Stud Extractor will help you get a grip on more easily repairing and replacing damaged hardware. The need to replace damaged equipment is stressful at the best of times, but expecting the unexpected by having the right tools makes all the difference. The adjustable design of this stud extractor eliminates the risk of misjudging which bolt extractor to use, leading to stripping your hardware even further. Just as you would attach a driver bit to your power drill, this stud extractor rotates to clamp securely on studs of all sizes, ensuring that you won’t have to try more than once. This tool is universal from end to end, with two attachment options that let you use the wrench, ratchet, or set of pliers that suits you best. Once your work is done, the knurling around the body of the extractor will keep you from losing your grip on your extractor. With the OEMTOOLS Universal Stud Extractor, you can always have it your way.

* * *

#### WARNING:

CHOKING HAZARD -- Small parts. Not for children under 3 yrs.

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

### Customers say

Customers find the stud extractor to be of great quality and easy to use, with one customer successfully removing six threaded studs from rusted exhaust manifolds. The tool's durability receives mixed feedback - while some say it's worth the money, others warn not to waste time or money on it, and several report it breaking during use. The grip and stability aspects also receive mixed reviews, with one customer noting the fingers that grab the stud are flimsy.

Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. ​If you are working on a 5.3L and dealing with the dreaded broken exhaust manifold bolts, stop looking and buy the OEMTOOLS 25438 Universal Stud Extractor immediately.

​I recently found myself in a nightmare scenario: a broken manifold bolt on the back passenger side of a 5.3L. If you've worked on these engines, you know that specific location is incredibly tight and usually practically impossible to get to without pulling the head or welding a nut to the stud, both of which take forever and cost money I didn't want to spend.

​I decided to give this extractor a shot, and it worked flawlessly.

​Why it earned 5 stars:

​Grip: It bit into the stud immediately. No slipping, no stripping.

​Size: It is compact enough to fit into that tight space on the back passenger side against the firewall.

​Efficiency: It turned a potentially multi-hour (or multi-day) ordeal into a quick fix.

​This tool saved me a massive amount of time, money, and frustration. It is rare that a tool works exactly as advertised in a worst-case scenario, but this one did. Highly recommended for any mechanic or DIYer tackling exhaust work. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. Worked well for the issue I had on my 351 5.8L ford Windsor, had 1 broken water pump stud preventing new water pump installation in my 92 bronco I had to remove the steering box & a/c component bracket in order to have space to get the extractor on the broken stub to pull out, once I had the space I sprayed some penetrating spray & used this tool in combination with a M12 stubby impact to allow the 3 jaws to clamp down on the tool and remove the frozen stud the whole process was straight forward & took about 15 minutes I would highly recommended this tool if you have the space to clear whatever you’re removing. # Product Summary: OEMTOOLS 25438 Stud Extractor Clamps 6mm to 12mm Bolts, Studs, Screws | Stud Extractor Tool, Manifold Stud Remover, Worn Screw Remover, Broken Stud Extractor, 3/8" Drive, 15/16" SAE Hex

## About this Item

*   Well Rounded: This rotating stud extractor ensures a picture perfect fit around the damaged stud; This stud remover tool can handle studs 1/4"—1/2” (6MM—12MM) in size
*   Fits Like a Glove: Turn to your stud remover when grappling with rusted bolts, stripped hardware, and chipped or broken nuts, BEST RESULTS WITH PENETRATING OIL OR HEAT
*   Any Which Way: The square drive of the stud removal tool fits manual wrenches or ratchets; If you prefer flat or adjustable sockets, pliers, or wrenches, the hex end is for you
*   Get a Grip: You won’t have to chase after runaway stud removers again; Knurling engraved on this bolt extractor will prevent your tools from slipping through your fingers
*   Product Details: Universal stud extractor fits tools with 1/4"—1/2” (6—12MM) diameters; 3/8” hex end drive for universal wrench or ratchet attachment

## Product Description

 The OEMTOOLS 25438 Universal Stud Extractor will help you get a grip on more easily repairing and replacing damaged hardware. The need to replace damaged equipment is stressful at the best of times, but expecting the unexpected by having the right tools makes all the difference. The adjustable design of this stud extractor eliminates the risk of misjudging which bolt extractor to use, leading to stripping your hardware even further. Just as you would attach a driver bit to your power drill, this stud extractor rotates to clamp securely on studs of all sizes, ensuring that you won’t have to try more than once. This tool is universal from end to end, with two attachment options that let you use the wrench, ratchet, or set of pliers that suits you best. Once your work is done, the knurling around the body of the extractor will keep you from losing your grip on your extractor. With the OEMTOOLS Universal Stud Extractor, you can always have it your way.
